Flagellum Daemonum (Girolamo Menghi, 1577)
==========================================================
Menghi's 1577 manual of exorcism, the most reprinted Catholic exorcist's handbook of the early modern period. Public domain. Digitized from Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/hin-wel-all-00002388-001).
